HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000166/B002","root_000532/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_012476de33c5e4576afc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:3:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B005\"]],[\"106:3:4\",\"root_000166\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ev ile Rab arasındaki ilişki, Kureyş'i beslenip korunan bir hane halkı olarak, Rabbi ise bu haneyi gözeten olarak düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000166/B008","root_000532/B001","root_000973/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_12427e50fbd41e79cfae","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:3:1\",\"root_000973\",[\"B006\"]],[\"106:3:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\"]],[\"106:3:4\",\"root_000166\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Buyruk, evin ve soyun itibarını bağımsız bir övünç olmaktan çıkarıp evin Rabbine yöneltir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2017fbf6181a7e00cbb6","text":"4. **Submission acquires the material image of a made-passable way.** Exploratorily, fixed `يَعْبُدُوا` activates `ع ب د B005` (physical subduing and smoothing, especially a traversable road). The preceding journey and the following removal of fear create a route-shaped structural frame around the imperative. This branch assigns worship the role of yielding resistance and becoming aligned, analogous to terrain made passable for repeated travel. The packet supplies the road branch, journey, and security; I infer the analogy submissive obedience -> traversability, since the fixed verbal form itself does not mean road-building. Worship then reads additionally as allowing the Lord's order to become an unobstructed way through seasonal risk.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2d4a5fac0f370f7d9df2","text":"5. **Honoring the house's Lord against prestige of the house.** Lexical evidence: ع ب د B006 (honoring/magnifying), ب ي ت B008 (lineage house of honor), س م و B008 in 106:0 (good repute spread), and ر ب ب B001 (lordship/ownership). Structural cue: \"رَبَّ هَٰذَا ٱلْبَيْتِ\" puts the Lord before the house, while the house carries potential tribal honor. Assigned role: the honor branches surface a possible prestige economy around house, lineage, and reputation. Abductive move: I infer that the imperative redirects honor attached to Quraysh/house toward the house's owner. Reading change: the ayah becomes a correction of status: the protected house is not an independent source of glory but a sign directing worship to its Lord.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000166/B001","root_000166/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2f8ab28956491a7d912e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:3:4\",\"root_000166\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Evin barınak ve gece tehlikesi çağrışımları, onun korumasının yolculuk güzergâhlarındaki korkuya karşı da uzandığını düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_42be35d82112c4bec8df","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:3:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Rablikte, ev çevresindeki güvenli düzeni bir antlaşma ve koruma güvencesiyle bir arada tutma boyutu da varsayılabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000532/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_45ac02e6a48709fe5e62","text":"5. **Lordship carries a covenantal protection layer.** Fixed `رَبَّ` also admits dormant `ر ب ب B011` (covenant, pact, and protection agreement), activated by familiar composition (`ء ل ف B002`) and final security whose `ء م ن B001` scope includes safe-conduct and trust. `هَٰذَا ٱلْبَيْتِ` supplies the stable locus around which that protected order coheres. The covenant branch's role is to bind travelers and house into reliable relations under the Lord; I infer that such binding is a mechanism of the stated security, because no human pact is named. This reading adds guarantorship and protected order to ownership without replacing `ر ب ب B001`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000166/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_53ffffec768e9609fccd","text":"3. **The house as shelter against route fear.** Lexical evidence: ب ي ت B001/B004 (shelter/dwelling; night action and attack), خ و ف B001/B002 in 106:4 (fear and intimidation), ء م ن B001 (place/situation of safety), and ر ح ل B007/B009 in 106:2 (dislodging; burdening with harm). Structural cue: the demonstrative \"هَٰذَا\" fixes the house locally after the seasonal routes; the last ayah names fear as removed. Assigned role: the house is the stable shelter that makes exposed travel non-threatening. Abductive move: I infer that the sanctuary's authority extends outward from a local house into route security. Reading change: \"this house\" reads as an anchor of safety, not merely an architectural or cultic reference.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_570d4a7ba7b507dbc926","text":"2. **`رَبّ` becomes stage-by-stage administration.** The fixed title activates dormant `ر ب ب B002` (repair, nurture, completion, and administration stage by stage). Seasonal alternation in `106:2` provides repeated stages, and `ٱلَّذِىٓ أَطْعَمَهُم ... وَءَامَنَهُم` in `106:4` supplies two concrete maintenance outputs. The relative construction assigns nurture to the Lord by describing him through those outputs. The packet explicitly links the relative clause grammatically; I additionally infer that repeated seasonal preservation is ongoing administration rather than two punctual acts. `رَبَّ هَٰذَا ٱلْبَيْتِ` thus reads as the maintainer who carries house and people through changing conditions, not authority as title alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7585cad751efc2d86ec5","text":"2. **Rabb as nurturer/completer of a house-centered people.** Lexical evidence: ر ب ب B002 (repair, nurture, completion), B005 (fostered child/caregiver relation), ب ي ت B002 (household and dependents), ر ح م B002/B003 in 106:0 (kinship/womb), and ط ع م B002 in 106:4 (feeding another). Structural cue: \"رَبَّ\" is linked by construct-like proximity to \"هَٰذَا ٱلْبَيْتِ\", and the final ayah concretizes nurture through feeding and securing. Assigned role: rabb is not only sovereign but caretaker of dependents affiliated with the house. Abductive move: I infer that the house gathers Quraysh into a household relation whose lord feeds and raises them. Reading change: worship becomes the response of dependents to nurturing lordship, not only subjects to power.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_77e24ec6f19d4410cab0","text":"4. **Submissive path-making instead of scattered self-direction.** Lexical evidence: ع ب د B005 (subduing/smoothing, a passable road), B010 (scattering in different directions), ء ل ف B002 (joining after separation), and ص ي ف B005 in 106:2 (turning aside from the aim). Structural cue: the command to worship follows the joined seasonal route; several branches contrast passability and deviation/scattering. Assigned role: worship functions like making the route \"mubaddad/muʿabbad\": smoothed, oriented, and not dispersed. Abductive move: I infer that obedient worship is the social-ritual analogue of a road made passable toward its proper center. Reading change: the ayah reads as re-channeling a mobile people away from dispersion and deviation into a single orienting service.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000166/B001","root_000166/B002","root_000166/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9bd9fdf9b721478a22e9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:3:4\",\"root_000166\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu ev, yalnızca bir yapı değil, mevsimlik hareketi çevresinde toplayan ve Kureyş'e ortak kimlik veren toplumsal bir merkezdir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_000973/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a250e4009f8623cf57ff","text":"The sole later ayah, `106:4`, changes all three fixed anchors. Its relative clause defines `رَبّ` by feeding and securing, strongly activating nurture/administration (`ر ب ب B002`) and, more tentatively, covenantal protection (`ر ب ب B011`). The same pair makes `بَيْت` function as shelter for bodily and anticipatory vulnerability, not status alone, and the removal of fear after a journey makes the passable-road image of `ع ب د B005` newly visible around the worship imperative. Benefaction -> obligation and worship -> traversability remain the inferred arrows.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a8de5503bbb3a0aefad8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:3:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rab unvanı, mevsimler boyunca besleme ve güvenliği sürdüren, topluluğu aşama aşama gözetip tamamlayan bir yöneticilik boyutu kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bfec7004b846a2eaeaf2","text":"1. **Worship is a response within a provisioned order.** Fixed `يَعْبُدُوا` activates `ع ب د B003` (worship and submissive obedience), fixed `رَبَّ` activates `ر ب ب B001` (lordship, ownership, and obeyed authority), and fixed `ٱلْبَيْتِ` activates `ب ي ت B001` (shelter and dwelling). The prefixed `فَـ` plus jussive imperative follows the familiar seasonal journey, while the next ayah's relative clause identifies the Lord through feeding and securing. Worship's assigned role is responsive service; lordship's role is the authority sustaining the conditions to which service responds. The syntax supplies consequence, but I infer the fuller benefaction -> obligation relation from the sequence. The command therefore reads as a response embedded in maintained livelihood and shelter, not as an isolated ritual instruction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ce78d336b3e191e1caff","text":"1. **Worship as repayment of stabilized circulation.** Lexical evidence: ع ب د B003 (worship and submissive obedience), ر ب ب B001 (lordship/ownership), ب ي ت B001 (shelter/dwelling), ء ل ف B005 from 106:1-2 (habituated attachment), and ر ح ل B001/B006 (journey/stage). Structural cue: the prefixed \"فَ\" ties the imperative to the preceding ilaf/rihlah sequence. Assigned role: the prior mobility supplies the reason; worship supplies the required response to the owner of the center. Abductive move: I infer that the command is generated by recognizing who makes the travel order possible. Reading change: 106:3 reads not as a detached ritual order but as the ethical consequence of a maintained seasonal system.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B005","root_000973/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d7346cf5359207b75bcb","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"106:3:1\",\"root_000973\",[\"B005\",\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kulluk, yolun düzeltilip geçilebilir kılınmasına benzer biçimde, dağınık yönelişlerin tek bir merkeze bağlanması olarak da okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000166/B001","root_000166/B008","root_000532/B002","root_000532/B005","root_000551/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ddb0d2b03dba62958c72","text":"106:4 retrospectively made \"رَبَّ\" more concrete than a title of authority. The feeding/security pair activates ر ب ب B002 and B005: lordship acts as nurture, completion, and care for dependents of a house. It also made \"البيت\" spatially outward-facing: not only shelter inside (ب ي ت B001), but a center whose protection extends into routes where fear could arise (خ و ف B001/B002; ر ح ل B007/B009). A smaller surprise is the prestige correction: after seeing \"أَطْعَمَهُم\" and \"ءَامَنَهُم\", the house's honor (ب ي ت B008) is not self-grounded; the final relative clause forces honor back toward the one who performs the sustaining acts.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000166/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f6023803ac6a482164b8","text":"3. **The house is both centripetal shelter and collective body.** Fixed `ٱلْبَيْتِ` activates `ب ي ت B001` (shelter and a place that gathers), `ب ي ت B002` (household and affiliated people), and `ب ي ت B008` (lineage house of honor). The deictic `هَٰذَا` fixes one nearby center after the outward seasonal `رِحْلَة`, while the plural recipients in `106:4` fill it with a social body. Shelter, household, and lineage respectively give the house spatial, social, and status roles. I infer that the seasonal movement is organized around this center; the packet supplies juxtaposition but no explicit return statement. The fixed phrase can therefore denote an institution that gathers and identifies Quraysh as well as a building that shelters them.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
